SCCW (NWC territory, 1999) — Card Archive

SCCW High Stakes — 25 January 1999

25 January 1999
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.

Stan stands tall, ready for his SCCW match, and possibly first step on long journey to the legendary NWC World championship. Stan pulls his left knee to his chest, then his right knee, stretching out, then holds up a hand. Alex: Good GOD! Manson: Maybe you don't understand… I am contending that he is on some sort of drugs, or out of his mind or something… I tell you what, I am gonna go get a close look.
Phil: Manson, please, sit down and… oh well… [Manson gets up out of his seat and walks towards the side of the ring that Kid
Crimson is at this time. Kid Crimson eyes him and glances at him, eyes catching eyes. Manson shakes his head like he just seen a ghost and walks back towards the announcing desk.]
Will: You satisfied? [Manson sits down calmly.] Manson (under his breath): That ain't Kid Crimson… Phil/Will: Ha ha ha… are you crazy? Alex: What makes you say that, Mr. Manson? Manson: That ain't Kid Crimson… Phil: Manson, you have lost your mind. Will: Prophet and Blade are trading punches while Armageddon is now choking
Kid… Alex: LOOK!!! LOOK!!! Phil: What? Will: What is your damn problem? Alex: Look at those tattoos under the sleeves of Kid Crimson… barbed wire? Phil: Alex, what is the significance? At least a half dozen SCCW wrestlers have those. Alex: Try and catch a glimpse on the other arm. Will: What? It is another tattoo, big deal… Manson: Read it, catch a close up and read it… Phil: What are you talking about? Will: I can't make it out… Phil: Blade and Prophet trying desperately to push each other out of the ring while Kid Crimson and Armageddon are both resting in opposite corners, both look exhausted.
Will: I can see why Armageddon is dead tired, but KC should have a little bit left. Phil: Maybe he is saving it. Alex: For what? Manson: Those two better not take their eyes off of Armageddon, he is a slippery, slimy slug… he will do anything to win this one…
Phil: Prophet with a rake to the eyes of Blade and Blade stumbles back… Prophet measures him…
[At the same split second, Armageddon and Kid Crimson both run at Crimson
Prophet and Blade respectively… Armageddon clotheslines Crimson Prophet over the top rope, and Kid Crimson sends Blade flying over the rope with a clothesline…]
Ring Announcer: The Crimson Prophet and Jason "Blade" Saxton have been eliminated… Will: NO!!! Manson: Damnit… Phil: We are down to two, and I don't know if they even realize it… [Armageddon and Kid Crimson are in the middle of the ring looking at the men they eliminated… both take a step back and bump the other. The slowly turn around to face each other as the crowd goes crazy…]
Phil: This is it… this is for the NWC Title Shot… this is why we are here…
Will: Listen to this crowd, they are electric… this is the loudest I have ever heard an SCCW crowd…
Alex: Is it possible? Kid Crimson, NWC Champion? Phil: I don't know, but… look! [At the top of the ramp, Donald "The Enforcer" Vaccaro, SCCW President has walked out with a puzzled look on his face. He slowly starts walking towards the ring, but stops halfway there.]
Phil: I do believe that Mr. Vaccaro is rather worried about this… he does not want Kid Crimson representing the SCCW in February…
Will: Just like always, he does not want to be embarrassed. Alex: And you do? Manson: I know who that is… I know who that man is… Phil/Will: Who? What man? Manson: Don't you clods listen to me? Just watch the damn match… Phil: Kid Crimson and Armageddon both facing each other now… Kid Crimson surprisingly showing no fear to the bigger Armageddon…
Will: KC looks like he has put on a little muscle the past few weeks… Manson: You guys amaze me… do the math poindexter… Alex: Yeah, do the math… huh? What math? Phil: Armageddon lunges at KC, but KC dodges him and slides to the side… trying to bait the big man to the ropes. Vaccaro watching this one intently… he has a lot at stake.
Will: Vaccaro keeps coming closer and closer, trying to get a good look, I guess. Alex: He pays the bills, he can stand anywhere he wants. Manson: Whatever. Phil: Vaccaro looks puzzled and it looks as if he is going to ringside and yelling something at Kid Crimson. Both men have stopped and are staring at Kid
Crimson.
Will: What is this all about? Alex: I could not tell you. Phil: Vaccaro is yelling at KC and motioning him to come over, and Kid Crimson is slowly coming over to the side Vaccaro is and leaning over the ropes listening to Vaccaro…
Will: Vaccaro wants him to take his mask off, he is making a motion for him to take his mask off…
Alex: Armageddon is getting a little peeved about this. Manson: He better watch it. Phil: Kid Crimson waves Vaccaro off and turns back towards Armageddon… Will: Armageddon runs at Kid Crimson. Alex: Clothesline! Manson: NOOOOOO!!!! Phil: Kid Crimson ducked… [As Armageddon rushes at Kid Crimson, KC ducks just enough to get under Armageddon and somehow push him up… and over the top rope… he lands on the apron… and tries to grab the rope, but instead falls to the concrete floor as the crowd erupts and a stunned Kid Crimson stumbles back in the ring with his hand raised…]
Phil: I don't believe it… Kid Crimson!!! Kid Crimson!!! Kid Crimson!!! Will: Listen to this crowd, they are going nuts! Crowd: CRIMSON!!! CRIMSON!!! CRIMSON!!! CRIMSON!!! Ring Announcer: Armageddon has been eliminated... Ladies and gentlemen, your winner… and next SCCW Challenger for the National Wrestling Council Heavyweight
Title… KIIIIID CRIIIIIMSSSSSSON!!!!!
Phil: Vaccaro smartly avoids Armageddon and starts making his way up the stairs and starts climbing in the ring…
Will: What is this going to be about. Manson: That is not Kid Crimson… Alex: Yeah, told you so… [Vaccaro calls for a mic, and is handed it by the ring announcer…] Phil: Vaccaro has to be livid at this… Vaccaro: Kid Crimson… as I can see, I have obviously made a very bad mistake, and for that I apologize…
[Vaccaro pulls something out of his back pocket. It looks to be a contract of some sort.] Vaccaro: KC, when Deacon pulled out of the Royal Rumble, you were announced as his replacement… everyone knew that, and my staff knew that…
Phil: What is he getting at? Vaccaro: But no one bothered to tell you that you had to have Deacon sign this form releasing his spot to you…
Will: You are bullshitting me, he can't do this… Vaccaro: And, as you see, KC, you obviously signed this contract, but since
Deacon did not sign this contract… you were not a legal participant in this
Royal Rumble… and for that, you shall be disqualified…
[The crowd boos louder than it ever has before. Vaccaro turns his back to Kid
Crimson and shows the crowd the legal document. Behind him, Kid Crimson bends over and reaches into his boot to pull out a folded up piece of paper. He taps
Vaccaro on the shoulder and hands him the paper, not saying a word. He motions
Vaccaro to read the paper.]
Phil: What now? Manson: This is crazy, I need to stay in Missouri from now on… [Vaccaro takes the document and reads it thoroughly… he has a puzzled look on his face and raises the mic to speak again.]
Vaccaro: Kid Crimson, I don't know what you are planned to pull… but this document has obviously been signed by Deacon… but that sure as Hell ain't your signature…
Phil: What the Hell is going on? Vaccaro: I can't even make it out. [Vaccaro continues to look at the signature. He turns his back to Kid Crimson, his face to the camera.]
Vaccaro: Hell, if I did not know better, I would say that signature looks like… Phil: Huh? [Vaccaro gets a sort of frightened look on his face. As he is about to say something else, Kid Crimson starts reaching back behind his head at the laces in his mask.]
Vaccaro: Yeah, if I did not know better, I would say that was Storm's signature… Phil/Will/Alex: WHAT??? [As he says this, Kid Crimson tears the mask off of his head to reveal none other than Rocky Storm!]
Manson: I KNEW IT! I F&%*ING KNEW IT!!! Phil: IT WAS ROCKY STORM! Alex: YES! Will: ROCKY WAS KID CRIMSON! [The crowd boo and throw trash, every possible emotion. Vaccaro realizes that something is going on and slowly turns around to be face to face with Rocky
Storm. Vaccaro jumps about four feet in the air and quickly slides out of the ring, fearful for his life…]
Phil: We are completely out of time… Rocky Storm was Kid Crimson… Rocky Storm has the document signed, sealed and delivered… Rocky Storm is going to face off for the NWC Heavyweight Title in February…
Will: We are out of time… <<The End>>
